## Classification Rules Covering All Files

- A KEEP_CURRENT: the 21 files listed under Current keep set below.
- B KEEP_REFERENCE: every file remaining in `00_CANONICAL/REFERENCE`.
- C KEEP_HISTORY: unique Changelog/Trace/Update/Archive/Report evidence, all moved Current snapshots, and every dirty/untracked path not explicitly listed for cleanup.
- D MERGE_THEN_DELETE: none; useful budget semantic content is merged before its source is moved to History, not deleted.
- E SUPERSEDED_DELETE: none; ambiguous superseded contracts are moved to History.
- F DELETE_NO_VALUE: byte-identical clean duplicate copies, obsolete reorganization inventory reports, and redundant Readable changelogs listed below.
